{\footnotesize\ttfamily void \mbox{\hyperlink{test__stackinit_8c_ace6f17999da4ea3b93c4227beafcab95}{\+\_\+\+\_\+user}}$\ast$ \+\_\+\+\_\+x86\+\_\+set\+\_\+memory\+\_\+region (\begin{DoxyParamCaption}\item[{struct \mbox{\hyperlink{structkvm}{kvm}} $\ast$}]{kvm,  }\item[{\mbox{\hyperlink{dcache_8h_ad65b480f8c8270356b45a9890f6499ae}{int}}}]{id,  }\item[{\mbox{\hyperlink{include_2linux_2kvm__types_8h_ad2e711a1b0fc0170bec01306c4a08a53}{gpa\+\_\+t}}}]{gpa,  }\item[{\mbox{\hyperlink{build_8c_a10e94b422ef0c20dcdec20d31a1f5049}{u32}}}]{size }\end{DoxyParamCaption})}

\+\_\+\+\_\+x86\+\_\+set\+\_\+memory\+\_\+region\+: Setup K\+VM internal memory slot

@kvm\+: the kvm pointer to the VM. @id\+: the slot ID to setup. @gpa\+: the G\+PA to install the slot (unused when @size == 0). @size\+: the size of the slot. Set to zero to uninstall a slot.

This function helps to setup a K\+VM internal memory slot. Specify @size $>$ 0 to install a new slot, while @size == 0 to uninstall a slot. \mbox{\hyperlink{classThe}{The}} return code can be one of the following\+:

H\+VA\+: on success (uninstall will return a bogus H\+VA) -\/errno\+: on error

\mbox{\hyperlink{classThe}{The}} caller should always use \mbox{\hyperlink{err_8h_a14e450a2ecb893a8c244a335097ba792}{I\+S\+\_\+\+E\+R\+R()}} to check the return value before use. Note, the K\+VM internal memory slots are guaranteed to remain valid and unchanged until the VM is destroyed, i.\+e., the G\+P\+A-\/$>$H\+VA translation will not change. However, the H\+VA is a user address, i.\+e. its accessibility is not guaranteed, and must be accessed via \+\_\+\+\_\+copy\+\_\+\{to,from\}\+\_\+user(). Here is the call graph for this function\+:
